**Weather Ball** (Japanese: **ウェザーボール** _Weather Ball_) is a damage-dealing [Normal-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normal_(type) "Normal (type)")
 [move](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Move "Move")
 introduced in [Generation III](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Generation_III "Generation III")
. Prior to [Generation IV](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Generation_IV "Generation IV")
, it was the [signature move](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Signature_move "Signature move")
 of [Castform](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Castform_(Pok%C3%A9mon) "Castform (Pokémon)")
. It was [TM46](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/TM46 "TM46")
 in [Pokémon Sword and Shield](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Sword_and_Shield "Pokémon Sword and Shield")
, and is [TM193](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/TM193 "TM193")
 in [Pokémon Scarlet and Violet](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Scarlet_and_Violet "Pokémon Scarlet and Violet")
.

Contents

Effect
------

Weather Ball deals damage. When the [weather](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Weather "Weather")
 is clear, Weather Ball is a [Normal-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normal_(type) "Normal (type)")
 move. During any type of weather except [strong winds](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Strong_winds "Strong winds")
, Weather Ball's [power](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Power "Power")
 doubles to 100. (Type-based power boosts from weather apply as normal, in addition to Weather Ball's effect.)

Weather Ball's type changes depending on the [weather](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Weather "Weather")
 at the time it is used.

* [Normal-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normal_(type) "Normal (type)")
     if there is no weather, it is [foggy](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Fog "Fog")
    , or there are [strong winds](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Strong_winds "Strong winds")
    .
* [Fire-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Fire_(type) "Fire (type)")
     if it is [sunny](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Harsh_sunlight "Harsh sunlight")
    .
* [Water-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Water_(type) "Water (type)")
     if it is [raining](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Rain "Rain")
    .
* [Ice-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Ice_(type) "Ice (type)")
     if it is [hailing](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Hail_(weather_condition) "Hail (weather condition)")
     or [snowing](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Snow "Snow")
    .
* [Rock-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Rock_(type) "Rock (type)")
     if there is a [sandstorm](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Sandstorm_(weather_condition) "Sandstorm (weather condition)")
    .

Weather Ball can be used as the second move of a [Pokémon Contest combination](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Contest_combination "Contest combination")
, with the user gaining 4 bonus appeal points if [Hail](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Hail_(move) "Hail (move)")
, [Rain Dance](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Rain_Dance_(move) "Rain Dance (move)")
, [Sandstorm](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Sandstorm_(move) "Sandstorm (move)")
, or [Sunny Day](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Sunny_Day_(move) "Sunny Day (move)")
 was used in the previous turn. It is also part of a Contest Spectacular combination, giving the user three extra appeal points if a weather summoning move was used in the prior turn.

### Generation III

Because damage category depends on the type of the move in Generation III, Weather Ball's category depends on its type. Unlike [Hidden Power](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Hidden_Power_(move) "Hidden Power (move)")
 where [Counter](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Counter_(move) "Counter (move)")
 always activates while [Mirror Coat](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Mirror_Coat_(move) "Mirror Coat (move)")
 never does, Weather Ball will activate Mirror Coat if it is a special move.

In [Pokémon XD: Gale of Darkness](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_XD:_Gale_of_Darkness "Pokémon XD: Gale of Darkness")
, when used during a [shadowy aura](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Shadowy_aura "Shadowy aura")
, Weather Ball's power doubles to 100, and the move becomes a [typeless](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Type#Typeless "Type")
 [physical move](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Physical_move "Physical move")
; as a typeless move, it bypasses [Wonder Guard](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Wonder_Guard_(Ability) "Wonder Guard (Ability)")
, deals regular damage against all Pokémon regardless of types, and the user does not receive [STAB](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Same-type_attack_bonus "Same-type attack bonus")
. If a Pokémon with [Color Change](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Color_Change_(Ability) "Color Change (Ability)")
 is hit by typeless Weather Ball, it becomes [Normal-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normal_(type) "Normal (type)")
.

### Generation IV

Weather Ball is affected by [Normalize](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normalize_(Ability) "Normalize (Ability)")
. It is now always a special move, so it is affected by [Mirror Coat](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Mirror_Coat_(move) "Mirror Coat (move)")
 but not [Counter](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Counter_(move) "Counter (move)")
.

### Generation V onwards

Weather Ball is unaffected by [Normalize](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normalize_(Ability) "Normalize (Ability)")
, [Pixilate](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pixilate_(Ability) "Pixilate (Ability)")
, [Refrigerate](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Refrigerate_(Ability) "Refrigerate (Ability)")
, [Aerilate](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Aerilate_(Ability) "Aerilate (Ability)")
, and [Galvanize](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Galvanize_(Ability) "Galvanize (Ability)")
. However, it is affected by [Ion Deluge](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Ion_Deluge_(move) "Ion Deluge (move)")
 (if the move would be Normal-type) and [Electrify](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Electrify_(move) "Electrify (move)")
.

Weather Ball has no effect on targets with [Bulletproof](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Bulletproof_(Ability) "Bulletproof (Ability)")
.

When used by a Pokémon with [Mega Sol](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Mega_Sol_(Ability) "Mega Sol (Ability)")
, Weather Ball will always be a Fire-type move regardless of the weather.

When used as a [Z-Move](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Z-Move "Z-Move")
 with [Normalium Z](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normalium_Z "Normalium Z")
, Weather Ball will be turned into [Breakneck Blitz](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Breakneck_Blitz_(move) "Breakneck Blitz (move)")
, but then Breakneck Blitz will turn into the Z-Move of the type corresponding to the type Weather Ball would be in that weather. This does not occur if Weather Ball is turned into a Z-Move via another move like [Sleep Talk](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Sleep_Talk_(move) "Sleep Talk (move)")
.

The same effect happens when Weather Ball is used as a [Max Move](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Max_Move "Max Move")
, displaying as [Max Strike](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Max_Strike_(move) "Max Strike (move)")
 but changing into the appropriate Max Move or G-Max Move corresponding to the type Weather Ball would be in the current weather.

Learnset

In other games
--------------

### Pokémon Mystery Dungeon series

Weather Ball deals damage. Its type and power changes depending on the weather.

In the [Rescue Team](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Red_Rescue_Team_and_Blue_Rescue_Team "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Red Rescue Team and Blue Rescue Team")
 series, Weather Ball is a [Normal-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normal_(type) "Normal (type)")
 in Clear and Cloudy weather; [Fire-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Fire_(type) "Fire (type)")
 in Sun; [Water-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Water_(type) "Water (type)")
 in Rain and Fog; [Ice-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Ice_(type) "Ice (type)")
 in Hail and Snow; and [Rock-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Rock_(type) "Rock (type)")
 in Sandstorm. Its power is increased by 100% in all types of weather except Clear and Cloudy. In the [Explorers](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Explorers_of_Time_and_Explorers_of_Darkness "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Time and Explorers of Darkness")
 series, Weather Ball no longer changes type or power in Fog.

From [Super Mystery Dungeon](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Super_Mystery_Dungeon "Pokémon Super Mystery Dungeon")
 on, Cloudy, Fog and Snow are no longer present in the game. The power boost in weather is 25%. Strong Winds and cataclysms do not affect Weather Ball.

### [Pokémon GO](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_GO "Pokémon GO")

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| [![](https://archives.bulbagarden.net/media/upload/thumb/c/c4/GO_sticker_lentilLaunch_camera.png/100px-GO_sticker_lentilLaunch_camera.png)](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/File:GO_sticker_lentilLaunch_camera.png) | **This section is missing one or more images.**  <br>Please feel free to add any missing images to this section. Needed images can be [uploaded](https://archives.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Special:Upload "a:Special:Upload")<br> to the Bulbagarden Archives. |

In Pokémon GO, the [Fire-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Fire_(type) "Fire (type)")
, [Ice-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Ice_(type) "Ice (type)")
, and [Water-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Water_(type) "Water (type)")
 Weather Ball moves are Charged Attacks that have been available since March 30, 2019. The [Rock-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Rock_(type) "Rock (type)")
 Weather Ball move is a Charged Attack that has replaced the [Normal-type](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Normal_(type) "Normal (type)")
 Weather Ball on May 31, 2021. On August 30, 2021, the Normal-type Weather Ball was added back to the game with updated stats. Unlike in the core series, Weather Ball's type is fixed and does not depend on the [weather](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Weather "Weather")
. The game's data instead stores its type variations as separate move entries.

The move's type and power changes with the weather. |

| **[SMD](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Super_Mystery_Dungeon "Pokémon Super Mystery Dungeon")<br>** | The weather determines its type and power.\*  <br>It damages a Pokémon, even one far away. The move's type varies, depending on the weather. The power goes up except when the weather is clear.\* |

_

Trivia
------

* Despite Weather Ball becoming a Rock-type move in a [sandstorm](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Sandstorm_(weather_condition) "Sandstorm (weather condition)")
    , Castform itself will not change form under these conditions.
* Prior to Generation IV, Weather Ball was one of the only two moves (along with [Hidden Power](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Hidden_Power_(move) "Hidden Power (move)")
    ) which could be either physical or special, due to the damage category being determined by the type.
